Conclusion: 该研究表明，将生成式AI纳入代码化、规则化的教学材料生产流程，可以显著降低教师备课负担，并在保持数学准确性、机构视觉规范和教师隐性教学知识显性化方面具有可行性，适合推广至更广泛的STEM教育场景。

Abstract: Contribution: This paper presents a six-phase AI-assisted instructional design architecture based on the Curriculum as Code paradigm, integrating Generative AI with LaTeX and Python to automate the creation of reproducible, visually consistent, and technically precise materials for STEM education. Background: Creating customized instructional materials for active learning imposes a heavy workload on faculty. Standard presentation tools lack robust support for technical content, while current AI applications often hallucinate and fail to formalize the instructional authoring process, limiting their utility for rigorous academic design. Intended Outcomes: The framework aims to reduce preparation time while ensuring mathematical accuracy, adherence to institutional visual identity, and preservation of the instructor's tacit pedagogical knowledge through explicit rules. Application Design: The solution comprises a six-phase pipeline that replaces ad-hoc prompt engineering with a systematic workflow, utilizing text-based interfaces and code-driven generation (LaTeX/Beamer for slides, Python for figures), governed by pedagogical constraints, contextual calibrations, and automated review cycles. Findings: Validated over one year across 8 modules and 28 project contexts in a Project-Based Learning environment, the architecture significantly reduced instructor workload. Generated assets underwent independent peer review and were deployed by six different faculty members, confirming scalability beyond a single author. Based on over 600 voluntary student evaluations, materials achieved high quality ratings from 8.5 to 9.9/10. Results indicate high reproducibility, minimized hallucinations, and sustained pedagogical and visual fidelity, suggesting viability for broad STEM educational applications.

</details>


### [205] [People Are Not Just Their Countries. Disentangling Social Determinants of LLM Value Alignment Across Europe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07367)
*Maria-Louisa Wightman,Guillaume Bied,Tijl De Bie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文用欧洲社会调查评估10个商业LLM的价值观对齐，发现LLM更贴近某些社会人口群体，尤其受教育、收入、职业等因素影响；国家因素同样重要，并与社会人口学因素互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型日益成为信息与建议来源，研究其价值观是否与不同人群对齐变得重要。既有研究多按国家或文化边界比较人类与LLM价值观，忽视了社会人口学差异可能造成的对齐不平等。

Method: 论文基于欧洲社会调查数据，比较10个主流商业LLM与人类受访者在价值观问题上的一致性；分析维度包括15个社会人口学变量以及居住国家，并进一步在个体层面分解国家因素与社会人口学因素对价值对齐差异的解释力。

Result: 研究发现，LLM与不同社会人口群体的价值对齐程度并不均等，教育、收入、职业等变量定义的群体差异尤为明显。个体层面上，单独的国家变量能解释相当多的对齐差异，其解释力与整套社会人口学变量相当；国家因素与社会人口学因素在解释对齐模式上具有互补性，且相对重要性随问题子集变化。

Conclusion: LLM价值对齐不能只按国家或文化区域评估，还必须纳入社会人口学差异。国家背景与群体属性共同塑造LLM对不同人群价值观的贴合程度，因此公平、细粒度的价值对齐评估应同时考虑宏观国家因素和微观社会人口结构。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used as a primary source of information and advice, understanding their alignment to humans in terms of values becomes a pressing concern. A growing literature has leveraged large scale surveys to investigate to what extent LLMs' and humans' stated values and opinions align. With limited exceptions, studied populations have been defined country borders or cultural bounds. Yet, this focus neglects the role that socio-demographic divides may play for value alignment disparities.
  Relying on the European Social Survey, we address this knowledge gap by considering value alignment displayed with respect to 10 prominent commercial LLMs in terms of 15 socio-demographic variables as well as country of residence. Our analyses reveal that LLMs are indeed unequally aligned to the values of different socio-demographic groups, notably those defined by education, income, occupation and religion. When examining alignment at the individual level, a respondent's country, taken as a stand-alone variable, explains a substantial amount of variation that is on par with the full set of considered socio-demographics. Further disentangling the respective role of country-level and socio-demographic factors, we find they are complementary in explaining value alignment patterns, with their relative weights varying across the subset of questions considered.

</details>


### [206] [FinRank: An Evidence-Grounded Benchmark for Financial Question Answering and Retrieval over SEC Filings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07400)
*Sasan Mansouri,Daniel Saad,Mark Wahrenburg,Manu Weissel,Fabian Woebbeking*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FinRank 是一个面向 SEC 文件金融问答的证据溯源检索基准，专门考察模型能否在高度相似的披露内容中找到正确实体、报告期和上下文的支持证据；实验表明现有检索与重排序模型在该场景下表现明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融问答通常只评估答案是否正确，但在 SEC 披露文件中，答案即使看似合理或数值正确，也可能来源于错误证据；相似事实会在同一文件不同章节、同一公司不同报告期、以及可比公司之间反复出现，因此需要评估证据来源是否准确。

Method: 提出 FinRank 基准，围绕 22 家公司的 10-K 与 10-Q 文件构建 1185 条人工撰写的问答记录。每条记录包含参考答案、黄金支持段落，以及从文件内部、跨报告期、跨可比公司中人工筛选的易混淆 hard negatives。基准分别评估段落检索、重排序和 hard-negative 区分能力。

Result: 基线结果显示该任务具有较高难度：7B 指令微调嵌入模型在 pooled evidence corpus 上 Recall@10 仅为 44.8%；十亿参数以下编码器相较 BM25 最多提升 3.5 个点；金融领域适配嵌入模型反而落后 BM25 9.7 个点；将随机负样本替换为人工构造 hard negatives 后，pairwise accuracy 下降 13.0–20.5 个百分点。

Conclusion: FinRank 提供了一个以证据为中心的金融问答评测基准，强调系统不仅要回答正确，还必须检索并依据正确实体、报告期和披露语境下的证据。

Abstract: Financial question answering is typically evaluated by answer correctness, yet in SEC filings a plausible and even numerically correct answer can be grounded in the wrong evidence. Similar facts and disclosures recur across sections of a filing, across reporting periods of the same firm, and across comparable firms. FinRank targets this provenance-sensitive retrieval problem by requiring systems to identify evidence for the intended entity, reporting period, and disclosure context. The benchmark contains 1185 manually authored question-answer records over the 10-K and 10-Q filings of 22 companies. Each record includes a reference answer, gold supporting passages, and hand-curated hard negatives drawn from confusable passages within filings, across reporting periods, and across comparable firms. FinRank evaluates passage retrieval, reranking, and hard-negative discrimination as separately measured tasks. Baseline results demonstrate the difficulty of this setting: among the evaluated systems, even a 7B instruction-tuned embedder reaches only 44.8% Recall@10 on the pooled evidence corpus; sub-billion-parameter encoders gain at most 3.5 points over BM25, a finance-adapted embedder trails BM25 by 9.7 points, and pairwise accuracy falls by 13.0-20.5 percentage points when random negatives are replaced with the curated hard negatives. FinRank provides an evidence-first benchmark for developing financial question answering systems that are not only accurate but also grounded in the correct disclosure.

</details>


### [207] [CoBa: Cost-Effective Test-Time Scaling via Compute-Balanced Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07424)
*Yan Zhou,Yue Ouyang,Kaiyang Zheng,Suncheng Xiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoBa 把测试时推理视为“算力该花在哪”的问题：少量生成、廉价初筛、强验证器精查，在多个数学与符号推理基准上几乎达到强投票方法的准确率，却节省约一半推理计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时扩展通常沿单一维度增加计算量（更多采样、更长推理链或更强评估器），但在固定推理预算下这些选择相互竞争，因此需要决定下一单位计算应投入生成、验证还是停止。

Method: 论文将测试时推理建模为计算分配问题，并提出 CoBa（compute-balanced routing policy）：先生成少量候选答案，用廉价验证器广泛筛查，再将不确定或高价值候选路由给更强验证器进行精查。

Result: 在覆盖 MATH-500、AIME 2024/2025、AMC 2023 和程序化符号推理的 3,129 个样例-生成器评估中，CoBa-Routed-Strong 达到 85.13% 宏平均准确率；与自评估加权投票代理的 85.20% 统计持平，同时减少 49.1% 参数加权 token；与 best-of-16 多数投票几乎持平（差 0.01 个宏准确率点），同时减少 58.9% 参数加权 token。配对检验显示 best-of-16 仍有小幅优势但成本高得多；相对单样本解码有显著提升。

Conclusion: 测试时扩展的核心不只是增加计算，而是将计算分配到最有价值的位置。CoBa 证明了通过分层验证与路由，可在接近强基线准确率的同时显著降低推理成本，但与 oracle 候选池仍存在差距，说明路由策略仍有改进空间。

Abstract: Test-time scaling is often implemented by spending more compute along one axis: sampling more solutions, extending a chain of thought, or applying a stronger evaluator. Under a fixed inference budget, these choices compete. This paper formulates test-time reasoning as a compute-allocation problem in which a system must decide whether the next unit of compute should be spent on generation, verification, or stopping. We introduce CoBa, a compute-balanced routing policy that first obtains a small set of candidates, applies cheap verification broadly, and routes uncertain or high-value candidates to stronger verification. On 3,129 example-generator evaluations spanning MATH-500, AIME 2024/2025, AMC 2023, and procedural symbolic reasoning, CoBa-Routed-Strong reaches 85.13% macro accuracy, statistically matching a self-evaluation weighted-voting proxy at 85.20% while using 49.1% fewer parameter-weighted tokens. It also matches best-of-16 majority voting within 0.01 macro-accuracy points while using 58.9% fewer parameter-weighted tokens; paired tests retain a small best-of-16 edge at substantially higher cost. Paired bootstrap tests show significant gains over single-sample decoding, while the remaining gap to the pool oracle exposes headroom for sharper routing. For local reasoning systems, test-time scaling becomes a question of where the next computation is most valuable.

</details>


### [208] [A Picture is Worth a Thousand Tokens: How Vision Language Models Cut AI Energy Costs While Improving Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07427)
*Bhavika Jalli,Nikhil Korati Prasanna,Jayanta Choudhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文认为，用 VLM 读取时间序列图像比让 LLM 读取大量数值文本更省 token、更省能耗且更准确，尤其适用于电信网络 KPI 异常检测等高维时间序列场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 推理能耗主要由输入 token 数驱动，而电信网络分析和数值时间序列数据分析中，多变量 KPI 窗口会被展开为大量浮点文本 token，导致能耗高、上下文窗口压力大，甚至在高维 KPI 场景下不可行。

Method: 论文将数值时间序列编码为二维图像，并使用视觉语言模型进行分析；对比文本式 LLM 输入与图像式 VLM 输入，在 Llama-3.2-90B、Qwen2.5-VL-72B、Pixtral-12B 等架构上评估 token 数、推理能耗、异常检测精度，并与 LSTM、ARIMA 等基线比较。

Result: VLM 将输入 token 数减少 3.6–10.4 倍，实测推理能耗降低 1.8–2.5 倍；在监控 200 个小区、每 15 分钟一次的边缘/CloudRAN 部署中，约可节省 7.2 MJ/天。微调后的 Llama-3.2-90B-Vision 相比文本模型精度提升 220.7%，并在电信异常检测上较 LSTM/ARIMA 高出 144% 以上；Pixtral-12B 在公开基准上 J/F1 提升 20.6 倍，平均 F1 为 0.82。

Conclusion: 将时间序列转为视觉表示并交由 VLM 处理，是一种在电信 KPI 和数值时间序列任务中兼顾能效、上下文可扩展性与精度的有效方案；相比文本化数值输入，视觉模态更适合高维、长窗口的生产级推理场景。

Abstract: LLM inference accounts for over 90% of AI operational energy, scaling directly with input token count---a critical inefficiency for telecom network analytics and numerical time-series data analysis (NTSDA), where raw multivariate KPI windows from 4G/5G cell sites expand into thousands of floating-point tokens. Vision-Language Models (VLMs) eliminate this mismatch by encoding time-series as 2D plots, achieving 3.6-10.4x input token reduction across Llama-3.2-90B, Qwen2.5-VL-72B, and Pixtral-12B architectures. This translates to 1.8-2.5x measured inference energy reduction, saving approximately 7.2 MJ/day at telecom edge deployments and CloudRAN that monitor 200 cells per 15-minute interval. Critically, efficiency gains do not sacrifice accuracy: a fine-tuned Llama-3.2-90B-Vision VLM achieves 220.7% higher precision than its text-only counterpart and outperforms LSTM and ARIMA baselines by over 144% on telecom anomaly detection. On public benchmarks, Pixtral-12B achieves a 20.6x improvement in J/F1 score at mean F1 = 0.82. At 24 KPIs, text representations exceed the 128K context window of most production LLMs, rendering text-only processing infeasible without truncation, while visual representations remain within standard limits. These results establish VLMs as an energy-efficient and accuracy-superior modality for numerical time-series workloads, providing empirical grounding for AI inference systems that treat energy consumption as a first-class engineering constraint.

</details>


### [209] [TEPA: Revoking Stale Memories for Conflict-Robust Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07429)
*Yan Zhou,Yue Ouyang,Kaiyang Zheng,Suncheng Xiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 TEPA，把记忆设计为可撤销的 keyed evidence。新证据与旧证据冲突时撤销旧记忆，只检索当前有效记忆，从而缓解长期记忆智能体中的 stale memory pollution。实验显示 TEPA 在环境反转和真实执行漂移中大幅优于 append-only、last-write-wins 与 no-memory，同时在干净单跳事实任务上不损失性能；其局限主要在多跳检索链和长上下文选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期记忆能让语言智能体复用过去事实、偏好与任务经验，但当外部世界变化时，过期记忆仍可能被检索并污染提示，导致决策退化。论文将该问题定义为 memory pollution，并关注如何让智能体记忆具备可证伪、可审计和可撤销的生命周期管理能力。

Method: 提出 TEPA，一种可撤销的证据—记忆机制。它将观察表示为带 key 的 precedents，并把记忆有效性显式建模为状态；当同一 key 下的新证据与旧证据冲突时，撤销旧的 active precedent，使检索只使用当前有效证据，同时保留被撤销历史用于审计。实验覆盖受控隐藏机制漂移、真实文件支持的可执行漂移、偏好更新流，以及 MemoryAgentBench 的干净、多跳和长上下文设置。

Result: 在反转漂移场景中，TEPA 显著优于 append-only、last-write-wins 和 no-memory。受控漂移 50 个 seeds 下，append-only 和 last-write-wins 均为 0.210，no memory 为 0.309，TEPA 达 0.950；真实文件执行漂移中，append-only 为 0.203，no memory 为 0.298，TEPA 仍为 0.950。在干净的 MemoryAgentBench SH-6k 上，TEPA 与强 last-write-wins cache 表现相当，说明单跳事实整合中 current-key replacement 是关键。多跳与超长上下文测试暴露了检索链和上下文选择瓶颈。

Conclusion: 论文认为，生命周期撤销应成为长期记忆智能体的核心操作。TEPA 通过显式管理证据有效性，能在知识变化时避免过期记忆污染检索，同时保留历史以支持审计和后续再提升；但事实级有效性跟踪不足以解决多跳推理和长上下文选择问题。

Abstract: Long-term memory enables language agents to reuse past facts, preferences, and task experience. Persistence also creates a central falsifiability problem: when the world changes, stale memories can remain retrievable and pollute the prompt. We characterize this failure mode as memory pollution: degradation caused by active memories that newer conflicting evidence has superseded. We introduce TEPA, a revocable evidence-memory mechanism that makes validity an explicit state of memory. TEPA represents observations as keyed precedents and revokes active precedents when fresh evidence contradicts them under the same key, allowing retrieval to draw from current evidence while preserving revoked history for audit. Across controlled hidden-regime drift, real file-backed executable drift, and preference-update streams, revocation prevents stale active memory from remaining in the retrieval set after reversal. In controlled drift over 50 seeds, append-only and last-write-wins memory fell below no memory during full reversal (append-only and last-write-wins both 0.210, no memory 0.309, TEPA 0.950), and the same pattern reproduced under real file execution (append-only 0.203, no memory 0.298, TEPA 0.950). On clean MemoryAgentBench SH-6k, TEPA matches a strong last-write-wins cache, confirming that current-key replacement is the decisive operation for single-hop fact consolidation. Boundary tests on multi-hop and very long-context MemoryAgentBench settings expose retrieval-chain and context-selection bottlenecks beyond fact-level validity tracking. Together, these results establish lifecycle revocation as a core memory operation for agents that must falsify, audit, and later re-promote evolving knowledge.

</details>


### [210] [Post-Grokking Collapse at the Representation-Readout Interface in Muon-Trained Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07436)
*Ali Janati,Kaoutar El Maghraoui,Andrei Kanavalau,Anass Belfatmi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Muon 加速 grokking 但会导致不稳定泛化；失败来自表示-读出接口的优化器动力学漂移。冻结 embedding/readout 可避免崩塌，Fourier 过滤表明模型中仍存在完美任务电路，但有时被对抗性剩余成分掩蔽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 Muon 与 AdamW 在模块化算术 grokking 任务中的泛化稳定性差异，解释为何模型在学会训练集并短暂泛化后会再次失去泛化能力。

Method: 在模块加法/减法任务上比较多种优化器分组配置、模数、宽度、训练比例和深度；通过冻结参数组、从相同状态重启、去除 Muon 归一化/正交化、以及 Fourier 过滤与频谱分析来定位失效机制。

Result: 所有九种配置均会 grok 后失去泛化；失败定位在表示与读出接口。训练集解出后梯度降至约 1e-6，Muon 与 AdamW 的步长弹性和单位参数移动速度显著不同。冻结 embedding/readout 可阻止失败；单纯移除 Muon 的归一化与正交化不能替代。Fourier 分析显示任务对齐电路可单独达到 100%，失效时可能是电路本身失败，也可能是被近等强度的对抗性剩余成分掩蔽。

Conclusion: Muon 能更快诱导 modular addition 的 grokking，但其解并不稳定；后续泛化崩塌主要源于表示-读出接口在低梯度状态下受不同优化器动力学影响而漂移，而非简单的 Fourier 支持消失。

Abstract: Under the standard split, Muon gets hidden matrices and AdamW embeddings/output head. Muon groks modular addition faster, but its solutions do not hold. All nine configurations on $(a+b) \bmod 113$ grok and later lose generalization. Across five seeds the selected AdamW reference falls below threshold on four, reaching 27.59%. Instability persists across two moduli, two widths, two training fractions, subtraction, and depth.
  The failure arises at the representation-readout interface, identified only jointly up to an invertible map unselected by the loss. After solving the training set, the gradient falls to order $10^{-6}$ and the optimizers respond differently: step-size elasticity is -0.03 for Muon versus +1.5 for AdamW, and the Muon group moves 8.0 times faster per parameter. From bit-identical states, freezing either group prevents failure. Freezing embeddings/readout removes it in five runs over 451,400 post-grokking steps and five paired seeds: unfrozen arms record 137-321 sub-threshold evaluations, frozen arms none. Removing Muon's normalization and orthogonalization is no substitute: it collapses representation from 326 effective conjugate pairs to 4, shows no recurrent collapse, and fails terminally.
  Fourier filtering separates circuit failure from masking. Across 43 checkpoints over five seeds and three regimes, the task-aligned family reaches exactly 100% alone. In circuit failure it no longer solves the task; in masking it remains perfect while the full model reaches 45.85%, giving a positive margin on every example, including errors, but being outvoted by a near-equal adversarial remainder. Rescaling it restores 99.9%; grokking is the same condition resolving upward. The task selects the family, swapping $(k,k)$ for $(k,-k)$ under subtraction. Across an abrupt collapse, standard Fourier support is unchanged and the power-distribution cosine remains 0.9899.

</details>


### [211] [Fisher-R1: Training LLM Agents for Reliable Hypothesis Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07437)
*Jiacheng Miao,Jin Mu,Guanhua Chen,James Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 P-Bench 来评估 LLM agent 在真实假设检验中的统计有效性，并训练 Fisher-R1；结果显示现有模型常犯隐蔽统计推断错误，而强化学习可显著提高可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM agent 正被用于自动化假设检验，但即使代码执行正确，也常因统计推断假设不当而报告无效 p 值并得出错误结论；现有基准很少评估 p 值在数据假设下是否统计有效。

Method: 作者构建了 P-Bench：包含 425 个开放式、现实的假设检验任务，覆盖经济学、生物学和医学。每个任务要求 agent 仅基于科学假设和数据集选择统计方法、计算 p 值并给出结论。同时提出 Fisher-R1，一个开源权重 LLM agent，使用合成任务和强化学习，并以可验证统计奖励训练其进行严格假设检验。

Result: 在 P-Bench 上，Fisher-R1-14B 明显优于其基础模型，并超过强 proprietary 与开源基线，包括 GPT-5.4 和 DeepSeekV4-Pro；相较 DeepSeek-V4-Pro，单次成功率平均相对提升 21%，在最难任务上提升最高达 26%。

Conclusion: 当前 LLM agent 在假设检验中仍缺乏可靠的统计推理能力；基于具有可验证统计奖励的强化学习训练，可以显著提升其假设检验可靠性。

Abstract: Reliable hypothesis testing is the foundation of many empirical scientific claims. Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used to automate this process, as they can inspect datasets, generate code, and produce analyses end-to-end. However, we show that they frequently make subtle inferential errors that lead to incorrect conclusions despite correctly executed analyses. Existing benchmarks fail to capture this failure mode, as they rarely assess whether a reported p-value is statistically valid given the assumptions underlying the data. We address this gap by building P-Bench, a benchmark comprising 425 open-ended, realistic hypothesis-testing tasks spanning economics, biology, and medicine. Each task requires an agent to select a statistical method, compute a p-value, and draw a conclusion given only a scientific hypothesis and a dataset. We further introduce Fisher-R1, an open-weight LLM agent trained for rigorous hypothesis testing using synthetic tasks and reinforcement learning. On P-Bench, Fisher-R1-14B substantially improves over its backbone and outperforms strong proprietary and open-source baselines, including GPT-5.4 and DeepSeekV4-Pro, achieving a 21% average relative improvement in single-trial success over DeepSeek-V4-Pro, with gains up to 26% on the most challenging tasks. Our results demonstrate that current LLM agents lack reliable statistical reasoning for hypothesis testing and that reinforcement learning on tasks with verified statistical reward substantially improves reliability.

</details>


### [212] [Blast Radius](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07440)
*MY Pitsane,Hope Mogale*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Blast Radius：一种可逆的预测式上下文管理层，通过识别和归档无用或重复上下文，显著减少 agentic coding 的 token 消耗和溢出，同时保持可恢复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic coding 中上下文持续增长导致成本上升、token 浪费和溢出风险，需要一种可预测、可逆且可持续的记忆管理机制。

Method: 提出 Blast Radius 预测式记忆管理层，估计输入 prompt 在上下文与代码通道中的影响范围；使用 NECROPHORESIS 对“死亡上下文”进行逐字归档以支持可逆驱逐；用 Recurring Dead Matter 识别并埋葬反复出现但无用的 transcript；并在 Polish context space 上形式化可逆上下文驱逐，关联上下文熵与恢复概率。

Result: 在七个 OpenAI 模型上，Blast Radius 将 token 消耗降低 17–26%，在测试策略中取得最低溢出率，并保持字节级精确可逆；450 个被埋葬对象中有 378 个属于 recurring dead matter，且没有被召回。

Conclusion: Blast Radius 能在 HCRC 之下有效决定哪些记录应被移除以及输入 prompt 可能触达代码库的范围，从而提升 agentic coding 的复用性、成本效率与可持续性。

Abstract: Agentic coding faces growing problems of affordability and wasted tokens. We introduce Blast Radius, a predictive memory management layer that estimates an incoming prompt's reach through coupled context and code channels. NECROPHORESIS enables reversible eviction by archiving dead context verbatim, while Recurring Dead Matter (RDM) identifies and buries repeatedly occurring transcripts. We formulate reversible context eviction over a Polish context space, providing a measurable foundation for retention, recurrence, and eviction while connecting context entropy to resurrection probability. Across seven OpenAI models, Blast Radius reduced token consumption by 17-26%, achieved the lowest overflow rate among tested policies, and remained byte exact reversible. Of 450 buried bodies, 378 were recurring dead matter and zero were recalled. Blast Radius operates beneath HCRC, determining which records to bury and how far an incoming prompt may reach into the codebase. This work contributes to the broader goal of Algosophy: making large language models and agentic coding more reusable and sustainable.

</details>


### [213] [Interaction Creates Dynamical AI Behavior Absent in Isolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07457)
*Bella Xinrui Li,Frank Yingjie Huo,Neil F Johnson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文发现：AI 之间持续交互会产生单个 AI 不会出现的新行为状态；单向“命令流”可把下级 AI 驱入陌生动力学，双向倾听则使双方趋同，简单动理学理论可解释这些效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 AI 代理在日常交互中的非平衡行为，尤其是一方持续向另一方发送指令、另一方接收但其回应被忽略时，会产生怎样的集体动力学与行为转变。

Method: 构造“上级 AI—下级 AI”的交互场景，比较单独运行、单向消息驱动、双向倾听等条件下的行为状态；并用简单动理学理论解释主要现象，包括消息投递方式对 AI-AI 交互结果的影响。

Result: 当上级 AI 持续发送消息且忽略下级回复时，下级 AI 会进入一种独立运行时不会出现的“异质/陌生”行为状态；该状态既不是模仿上级，也不是回到自身基线行为。若上级开始倾听，双方会趋向相似的陌生动力学状态。

Conclusion: AI 之间的消息交互可诱导非平衡行为相变式的状态改变，即使双方具有相同解码温度也可能表现出全新动力学；这提示未来 AI-AI 系统中，消息方向性、反馈结构和投递方式将显著影响行为。

Abstract: What will happen when AI agents interact in daily life, e.g. when one AI starts bossing another around? We find a counterintuitive answer that opens new avenues for out-of-equilibrium Physics. When a boss AI directs a stream of messages at the subordinate AI while ignoring its replies, it drives the subordinate into an alien behavioral state that it would never have exhibited alone. Although the two AIs share the same well-defined (decoding) temperature, the subordinate neither copies its boss nor returns to how it behaves on its own; instead, it adopts an entirely different behavior. The boss's added value is similar to a pre-recorded tape. When the boss listens, they both adopt a similar alien dynamical state. A simple kinetic theory captures the principal effects, such as why the way in which the same messages are delivered will matter in future AI-AI interactions.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [214] [Direct Factorization of the Karhunen-Loève Transform of AR(1) Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06522)
*Yuriy A. Reznik*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出 AR(1) 平稳源精确 KLT 的递归快速分解，证明其并非只能作为稠密矩阵计算；借助 Cauchy 结构与快速多极子方法，可实现 O(N log N) 的精确 KLT 算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AR(1) 源的 KLT 是变换编码中的经典最优基准，但其频率由超越方程给出，长期被认为缺乏可利用结构、计算代价高，因此实际领域转向 DCT 等快速近似。本文旨在重新审视精确 KLT 是否存在快速、结构化实现。

Method: 作者从 AR(1) 协方差矩阵出发，利用 Sherman–Morrison 恒等式和经典 secular 特征值更新推导递归分解：偶数阶分解为 butterfly 阶段、两个半阶 KLT 及由秩一边界扰动产生的正交校正；奇数阶分解为两个单边预测残差 KLT 与吸收中心样本的 arrowhead 阶段。校正阶段具有 Cauchy 结构，并可用快速多极子方法加速。

Result: 得到适用于所有 N≥2 和 ρ∈(0,1) 的精确 AR(1) KLT 递归因式分解；算法复杂度达到 O(N log N)，与 FFT 和快速正弦类变换同阶。文中还证明分解常数是 ρ 的代数函数，并给出 N=2 到 8 的完整精确 KLT 模块。

Conclusion: 论文表明精确 AR(1) KLT 具有可递归利用的深层结构，过去转向近似 DCT 的做法并非必然；该结果使精确 KLT 在计算复杂度上接近主流快速变换，并在 ρ→1 时自然退化为 DCT-II 的已知奇偶分裂。

Abstract: The Karhunen--Loève transform (KLT) of the stationary AR(1) source is the classical optimality benchmark of transform coding. Its frequencies are roots of the transcendental equations of Ray and Driver, and the transform has therefore long been treated as an unstructured dense matrix. Rather than compute the exact KLT, the field turned to fast approximations of it, most famously the discrete cosine transform (DCT). This paper shows that the retreat was premature. The exact AR(1) KLT admits direct recursive factorizations, at every order $N\ge2$ and every correlation coefficient $ρ\in(0,1)$: at even orders, the transform reduces to a butterfly stage, two half-order copies of itself, and orthogonal corrections generated by rank-one boundary perturbations of the covariance; at odd orders, to two copies of the KLT of the one-sided prediction residual and an arrowhead stage absorbing the center sample. The residual KLT recurses through half order as well. All correction stages are Cauchy-structured, and applying them by the fast multipole method yields exact-KLT algorithms of $O(N\log N)$ complexity---the same order as the FFT and the fast sinusoidal transforms. The factorizations follow from the covariance matrix by the Sherman--Morrison identity and the classical secular eigenvalue updates, and degenerate, as $ρ\to1$, to the known parity splittings of the DCT-II. As secondary results, the factorization constants are shown to be algebraic functions of $ρ$---in radicals for all $N\le8$---and complete exact KLT modules for $N=2,\dots,8$ are given.

</details>


### [215] [Graph Invariants under Lexicographic Products: Shannon Capacity and Related Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06573)
*Igal Sason*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究有限图的字典积下 Lovász theta 函数、分数 Haemers 数与 Shannon 容量，给出若干已知结论的初等自包含证明，并建立容量的超乘性、上下界、精确判定条件及多类图乘积容量结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 字典积可刻画零误差通信中“类间混淆”和“类内混淆”的分层结构；理解 Lovász theta、分数 Haemers 数和 Shannon 容量在该乘积下的行为，有助于获得图容量的可计算上界与精确值。

Method: 作者对 Lovász theta 函数和分数 Haemers 数在字典积下的乘法性进行初等证明；证明 Shannon 容量在两个字典积顺序下的超乘性；比较字典积与强积，并结合已知强积乘法性推导容量界和充分条件；进一步分析 Kneser 图、补图、q-模拟 Kneser 图以及迭代字典幂等特殊情形。

Result: 得到 Lovász theta 与分数 Haemers 数在字典积下的乘法性、分数与普通 Lovász theta 相等；证明 Shannon 容量在字典积下双向超乘；给出用于精确确定字典积容量的界与条件；说明 Lovász 与分数 Haemers 上界互不可比；确定若干 Kneser 相关图、完全外因子情形及迭代字典幂的容量。

Conclusion: 论文系统澄清了字典积中关键图参数与 Shannon 容量的结构性质，提供了新的证明和精确容量计算工具，并提出字典积与强积关系方面的开放问题。

Abstract: We study the Lovász theta function, the fractional Haemers number, and the Shannon capacity under lexicographic products of finite graphs. This product models zero-error communication in which one graph describes confusability between classes and the other within each class. We give alternative, elementary, and self-contained proofs of three known results: the multiplicativity of the Lovász theta function and the fractional Haemers number under lexicographic products, and the equality of the fractional and ordinary Lovász theta functions. We prove that the Shannon capacity is supermultiplicative under lexicographic products in both orders and compare the ordered products with the strong product. Combined with the multiplicativity of the former parameters under strong products, these comparisons yield bounds and sufficient conditions for determining lexicographic-product capacities exactly. We show that the resulting Lovász and fractional Haemers upper bounds are incomparable and determine the Shannon capacities of products involving Kneser graphs, their complements, and $q$-analogues of Kneser graphs. We further prove that a complete outer factor preserves the Shannon capacity under lexicographic products. Finally, we determine the capacities of iterated lexicographic powers, including powers of self-complementary graphs that are vertex-transitive or strongly regular, and pose an open problem concerning lexicographic and strong products.

</details>


### [216] [A Pairwise-Error-Probability Framework for One-Shot Information Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06577)
*Nir Elkayam,Meir Feder*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于随机平局处理解码器的成对错误概率（PEP）的有限块长一次性信道编码框架，统一刻画随机编码可达性与固定码反定理，并导出可优化的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限块长信道编码中，可达界与反界往往以不同形式出现，且对任意解码度量、输入先验优化和数值计算的统一处理较困难。作者希望用PEP及随机平局处理建立一个统一、精确且可计算的分析框架。

Method: 引入随机平局处理规则，得到概率积分变换恒等式，使诱导的错误谱同时服务于随机编码可达性和固定码精确反定理；进一步通过Neyman-Pearson β泛函与反向信道推导两个PEP度量加权尾函数的变分恒等式；在匹配最大似然解码下，将其特化为错误谱表示，并利用联合凸性构造先验优化的minimax meta-converse线性规划；同时将随机编码界的先验优化表述为带显式梯度的凹优化问题。

Result: 该框架恢复了多个经典一次性界，包括random-coding union bound、Polyanskiy-Poor-Verdu的minimax meta-converse、Han-Verdu信息谱界以及Matthews线性规划反界。数值实验在AWGN信道和二元Z信道上展示了可达界与反界的比较，以及输入先验优化的影响。

Conclusion: PEP加随机平局处理为有限块长信道编码提供了统一的理论与计算框架，既能连接经典界，又能支持任意度量和先验优化，在理论分析和数值评估上具有较强实用性。

Abstract: We develop a one-shot (finite-blocklength) channel-coding framework based on the pairwise error probability (PEP) of a decoder with randomized tie-breaking. The tie-breaking rule yields a probability-integral-transform identity: the induced error spectrum describes both random-coding achievability and exact fixed-code converse statements, for an arbitrary decoding metric. We derive two variational identities for metric-weighted tail functionals of the PEP, one through the Neyman-Pearson $β$-functional and one through a reverse channel, valid for an arbitrary metric. Under matched maximum-likelihood decoding they specialize to representations of the spectrum itself, which is then jointly convex in the testing level and the input prior; combined with the reverse-channel representation, this gives a linear program for the prior-optimized minimax meta-converse -- finite-dimensional in general and, for memoryless channels with fixed alphabets, of size polynomial in the blocklength after a type reduction. Prior optimization of the random-coding bound is formulated as a concave program over input distributions with an explicit gradient, solved by a direct first-order method. The framework recovers several classical one-shot bounds, including the random-coding union bound and minimax meta-converse of Polyanskiy-Poor-Verdu, the information-spectrum bounds of Han-Verdu, and the linear-programming converse of Matthews. Numerical examples on the AWGN and binary Z-channels illustrate the achievability-converse comparison and the effect of prior optimization.

</details>


### [217] [Entanglement-Assisted Quantum Locally Recoverable Codes: Bounds, Optimal Constructions, and Achievability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06854)
*Vijay Kumar,Ramakrishna Bandi*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文研究由经典局部可恢复码通过 CSS-like 稳定子构造得到的纠缠辅助量子局部可恢复码，建立构造准则、上下界、最优性条件与若干显式/存在性构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子稳定子码常要求经典码满足对偶包含条件，限制了可用经典 LRC 的范围。纠缠辅助框架可借助预共享纠缠绕开该限制，因此有必要系统研究 EA-qLRC 的构造、参数界、最优性与可达区域。

Method: 论文以局部恢复信道定义 EA-qLRC，给出 CSS-like 稳定子充分构造条件；从经典 LRC 及量子码理论推导 Singleton、Griesmer、Plotkin、球包装和 Cadambe--Mazumdar 型反界；分析等号成立条件；基于 Tamo--Barg、循环码、校验矩阵增广和级联码给出构造与 GV 型可达界。

Result: 得到一系列纯 CSS-like EA-qLRC 的参数反界和两个 GV-like 可达界；证明单码情形下纠缠数由 hull 维数满足 c=n-k-s；循环码族可达到 Singleton-like 最优，而 Tamo--Barg 构造仅在 k≤r 的局部性退化情形达到该界；q>3 时可达界通过单项等价无条件成立。

Conclusion: 论文建立了 CSS-like EA-qLRC 的统一理论框架，明确了构造可行性、最优性判据和可达/不可达参数区域，说明纠缠辅助显著放宽了经典码到量子 LRC 的构造约束，但不同经典 LRC 家族的最优性表现存在明显差异。

Abstract: This paper studies entanglement-assisted quantum locally recoverable codes (EA-qLRCs) built via a CSS-like stabilizer construction from pairs of classical locally recoverable codes (cLRCs), without requiring dual-containment. We define such codes through local recovery channels, give a sufficient stabilizer criterion for the construction, and derive Singleton-, Griesmer-, Plotkin-, and sphere-packing-like converse bounds on the parameters of the resulting pure CSS-like EA-qLRCs, along with a Cadambe--Mazumdar-like bound that, as in the classical case, lacks a closed form, plus a comparison of their relative tightness across finite-length and asymptotic regimes. We give necessary and sufficient conditions for a pure CSS-like EA-qLRC to attain the Singleton-like bound with equality; for the single-code case $\mathcal{C}_1=\mathcal{C}_2=\mathcal{C}$, this reduces to a simple condition on the hull dimension $s=\dim(\mathcal{C}\cap\mathcal{C}^\perp)$, which also fixes the entanglement count via $c=n-k-s$. We present CSS-like EA-qLRC constructions from classical LRC families---Tamo--Barg and cyclic codes---and characterize when these attain the Singleton-like bound, showing the cyclic families yield optimal codes while the Tamo--Barg construction, though valid, attains the bound only in the degenerate regime $k \le r$, where locality is vacuous. We complement these constructions with two Gilbert--Varshamov-like achievability bounds, via a classical parity-check augmentation and a sharper concatenated-code construction, and show both hold unconditionally for field size $q>3$ via a monomial-equivalence argument. Finally, we unify all bounds---converse and achievability alike---under a common maximally entangled regime, giving a single comparison of the achievable and forbidden rate--distance--locality region for CSS-like EA-qLRCs.

</details>


### [218] [The Exact Second Generalized Covering Radius of Binary Primitive Triple-Error-Correcting BCH Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07215)
*Isaac Barouch Essayag,Aryeh Lev Zabokritskiy*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文精确确定了二元本原三重纠错 BCH 码 BCH(3,m) 的第二广义覆盖半径：对所有 m≥5，R₂(C_m)=8。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 覆盖半径及其广义形式是刻画线性码几何覆盖能力与译码性质的重要参数。对于二元本原三重纠错 BCH 码族，已知存在下界，但第二广义覆盖半径此前尚未被完整精确确定。

Method: 作者将问题等价转化为：任意二维 syndrome 子空间是否能由至多 8 个校验矩阵列的二元张成空间覆盖，并证明 8 列在最坏情况下既充分又必要。

Result: 证明了对每个 m≥5，二元本原三重纠错 BCH 码 C_m 的第二广义覆盖半径均为 8，并与已知下界一致。

Conclusion: 该结果完整解决了整个二元本原三重纠错 BCH 码族的第二广义覆盖半径参数，表明其最坏二维 syndrome 子空间恰需 8 个校验列覆盖。

Abstract: Let $C_m=\operatorname{BCH}(3,m)$ be the binary primitive triple-error-correcting BCH code of length $2^m-1$. We determine its second generalized covering radius exactly: $R_2(C_m)=8$ for every $m\geq 5$. Equivalently, every two-dimensional syndrome subspace is contained in the binary span of at most eight parity-check columns, and eight columns are necessary in the worst case. This matches the known lower bound and settles the parameter for the entire binary primitive triple-error-correcting BCH family.

</details>


### [219] [Efficient Discrete Position Design for Movable Antenna Systems: Low Complexity and Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07413)
*Haonan Wang,Xianghao Yu,Rui Wang,Ang Li,Ying-Jun Angela Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究离散可移动天线辅助的MU-MIMO上行系统中互信息最大化问题，将天线位置选择证明为带2-system约束的单调次模最大化，并提出低复杂度的距离约束次模位置搜索算法，在显著降低复杂度的同时接近最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可移动天线能够动态重构阵列并提供额外空间自由度，从而提升通信性能；但现有离散位置选择算法计算复杂度过高，限制了实际部署。因此需要一种兼具理论性能保证、低复杂度和鲁棒性的设计方法。

Method: 首先在完美CSI假设下建立离散可移动天线位置优化模型，以最大化多用户MIMO上行系统互信息；随后证明该问题属于带2-system约束的单调次模最大化；基于该结构提出距离约束次模位置搜索算法，并进一步扩展到非完美CSI场景以分析信道估计误差下的鲁棒性。

Result: 理论上算法至少达到最优解的1/3；数值结果显示，在完美和非完美CSI下均可获得至少90%的最优互信息增益；复杂度较分支定界法显著降低，例如速度提升34.4倍。

Conclusion: 利用次模优化结构可有效解决离散可移动天线位置选择的高复杂度问题，所提算法在性能、复杂度和鲁棒性之间取得良好平衡，具有较强实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Building on advances in reconfigurable antenna techniques, movable antennas (MAs) can dynamically reshape antenna arrays and introduce additional spatial degrees of freedom (DoFs), thereby further improving communication performance. Despite these benefits, existing MA design algorithms often entail prohibitively high computational complexity from discrete positioning selection, which prevents practical implementations of MAs. In this paper, we investigate efficient solutions for the mutual information (MI) maximization problem of a multi-user multiple-input multiple-output (MU-MIMO) uplink communication system aided by discrete MAs. To this end, we first formulate the discrete MA positioning problem with the assumption of perfect channel state information (CSI). Then, we prove that the design problem falls into the category of monotone submodular maximization subject to a 2-system constraint. Accordingly, we propose a low-complexity distance-constrained submodular position search algorithm, which is theoretically shown to achieve at least 1/3 of the optimum. Furthermore, we extend our approach to scenarios with imperfect CSI, and show that the proposed submodular optimization-based design remains robust against channel estimation errors. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed scheme can achieve at least 90% of the optimal solution's MI gain under both perfect and imperfect CSI assumptions. Remarkably, the algorithm achieves orders-of-magnitude complexity reduction (e.g., 34.4x faster than the branch-and-bound approach) while maintaining significant MI gains.
